```css
.game-board {
  min-height: 100vh;
  padding: 20px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  color: white;
}

.game-header {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #64b5f6;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.3);
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 20px;
}

.level-info h2 {
  color: #81d4fa;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.8);
}

.progress-bar {
  width: 200px;
  height: 8px;
  background: #222222;
  border: 1px solid #ce93d8;
  border-radius: 4px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.progress {
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ab47bc 0%, #64b5f6 100%);
  transition: width 0.5s ease;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.6);
}

.live-stats {
  display: flex;
  gap: 24px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.stat {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  background: #111111;
  border: 1px solid #ce93d8;
  border-radius: 8px;
  min-width: 80px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.2);
}

.stat-value {
  display: block;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #81d4fa;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.8);
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #ffffff;
  margin-top: 4px;
}

.game-content {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 250px 1fr 250px;
  gap: 20px;
  height: calc(100vh - 140px);
}

.tool-palette {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #ce93d8;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.3);
}

.tool-palette h3 {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #ce93d8;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.tools {
  display: grid;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
}

.tool {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 12px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
  background: #111111;
  color: white;
}

.tool.unlocked {
  border-color: #64b5f6;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.3);
}

.tool.unlocked:hover {
  background: #222222;
  border-color: #ce93d8;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.5);
}

.tool.locked {
  background: #0a0a0a;
  opacity: 0.4;
  cursor: not-allowed;
  border-color: #333333;
}

.tool span:first-child {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.tool span:last-child {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.next-level-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #66bb6a 0%, #4fc3f7 100%);
  color: #000000;
  font-weight: 700;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 14px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid #66bb6a;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.4);
}

.next-level-btn:hover {
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.6);
}

.city-block {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #64b5f6;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.3);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.city-block h3 {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #81d4fa;
  text-align: center;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.8);
}

.block-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
  flex-grow: 1;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.grid-item {
  background: #111111;
  border: 2px dashed #666666;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 20px;
  text-align: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  color: white;
}

.grid-item:hover {
  border-color: #ce93d8;
  background: #222222;
  transform: scale(1.02);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.4);
}

.grid-item.road {
  background: #2d1b1b;
  border-color: #ef5350;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.3);
}

.grid-item.parking {
  background: #2d2517;
  border-color: #ffa726;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(255, 167, 38, 0.3);
}

.grid-item.shops {
  background: #1b2d1b;
  border-color: #66bb6a;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.3);
}

.grid-item.bus-stop {
  background: #1a1f2e;
  border-color: #42a5f5;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(66, 165, 245, 0.3);
}

.grid-item.bike-lane {
  background: #1b2d23;
  border-color: #4fc3f7;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(79, 195, 247, 0.3);
}

.grid-item.mixed-use {
  background: #221b2d;
  border-color: #ba68c8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(186, 104, 200, 0.3);
}

.grid-item span:first-child {
  font-size: 2rem;
}

.grid-item span:last-child {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.animations {
  border-top: 1px solid #ce93d8;
  padding-top: 16px;
}

.people-animation {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
  justify-content: center;
}

.person {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

.hints-panel {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #ce93d8;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.3);
}

.hints-panel h3 {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #ce93d8;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.hint {
  background: #111111;
  border: 1px solid #4fc3f7;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #4fc3f7;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  line-height: 1.4;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(79, 195, 247, 0.2);
}

.complete-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ab47bc 0%, #64b5f6 100%);
  color: #000000;
  font-weight: 700;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 14px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid #ab47bc;
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.5);
}

.complete-btn:hover {
  box-shadow: 0 0 35px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.7);
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .game-content {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    height: auto;
  }
  
  .game-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    text-align: center;
  }
  
  .live-stats {
    justify-content: center;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .block-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .live-stats {
    gap: 12px;
  }
  
  .stat {
    min-width: 60px;
    padding: 8px 12px;
  }
}
```

```css
.game-results {
  min-height: 100vh;
  padding: 20px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  color: white;
}

.results-header {
  text-align: center;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  padding: 40px 20px;
}

.results-header h1 {
  font-size: 3rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #81d4fa;
  text-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(129, 212, 250, 1);
}

.results-header .subtitle {
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  color: #ce93d8;
  margin-bottom: 0;
  text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.8);
}

.comparison-section {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #64b5f6;
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 40px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 0 50px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.4);
}

.before-after {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr auto 1fr;
  gap: 40px;
  align-items: center;
}

.comparison-column {
  text-align: center;
}

.comparison-column h2 {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.comparison-column:first-child h2 {
  color: #ef5350;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.8);
}

.comparison-column:last-child h2 {
  color: #66bb6a;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.8);
}

.city-visual {
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 24px;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
  border: 2px solid;
}

.city-visual.before {
  border-color: #ef5350;
  background: #2d1b1b;
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.3);
}

.city-visual.after {
  border-color: #66bb6a;
  background: #1b2d1b;
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.3);
}

.visual-item {
  background: #111111;
  border: 1px solid #333333;
  padding: 12px;
  margin: 8px 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.stats-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: 16px;
}

.stat-item {
  background: #111111;
  border: 1px solid #333333;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  text-align: center;
  position: relative;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.stat-item.improved {
  background: #1b2d1b;
  border: 2px solid #66bb6a;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.4);
}

.stat-item .number {
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  display: block;
  color: #ffffff;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
}

.stat-item.improved .number {
  color: #66bb6a;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.8);
}

.stat-item .label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #cccccc;
  display: block;
  margin-top: 4px;
}

.stat-item .improvement {
  position: absolute;
  top: -8px;
  right: -8px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #66bb6a 0%, #4fc3f7 100%);
  color: #000000;
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(102, 187, 106, 0.6);
}

.vs-arrow {
  font-size: 3rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #ce93d8;
  text-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(206, 147, 216, 1);
}

.key-insights {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #ce93d8;
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 40px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.4);
}

.key-insights h2 {
  text-align: center;
  margin-bottom: 32px;
  font-size: 2rem;
  color: #ce93d8;
  text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.8);
}

.insights-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr));
  gap: 24px;
}

.insight-card {
  background: #111111;
  border: 1px solid #64b5f6;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border-left: 4px solid #64b5f6;
  transition: transform 0.3s ease;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.2);
}

.insight-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-4px);
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.4);
  border-color: #ce93d8;
  border-left-color: #ce93d8;
}

.insight-card h3 {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
  color: #81d4fa;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.6);
}

.insight-card p {
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.share-section {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  border: 2px solid #ce93d8;
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 40px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.3);
}

.share-section h2 {
  margin-bottom: 24px;
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  color: #ce93d8;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.8);
}

.share-text {
  background: #111111;
  border: 1px solid #64b5f6;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
  text-align: left;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.2);
}

.share-text p {
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: white;
}

.share-text p:last-child {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

.share-buttons {
  display: flex;
  gap: 16px;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.share-btn {
  padding: 12px 24px;
  border-radius: 25px;
  font-weight: 600;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid;
}

.share-btn.twitter {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #42a5f5 0%, #64b5f6 100%);
  color: #000000;
  border-color: #64b5f6;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(100, 181, 246, 0.4);
}

.share-btn.facebook {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #5c6bc0 0%, #ab47bc 100%);
  color: white;
  border-color: #ab47bc;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.4);
}

.share-btn.copy {
  background: #111111;
  color: #81d4fa;
  border-color: #81d4fa;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.3);
}

.share-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.action-buttons {
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.restart-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ab47bc 0%, #64b5f6 100%);
  color: #000000;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 16px 32px;
  border-radius: 25px;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  border: 2px solid #ab47bc;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.6);
}

.learn-more-btn {
  background: #111111;
  color: #81d4fa;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 16px 32px;
  border-radius: 25px;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  border: 2px solid #81d4fa;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.4);
}

.restart-btn:hover {
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.8);
}

.learn-more-btn:hover {
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(129, 212, 250, 0.6);
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .before-after {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 24px;
  }
  
  .vs-arrow {
    transform: rotate(90deg);
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .insights-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .results-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .comparison-section {
    padding: 24px;
  }
  
  .key-insights {
    padding: 24px;
  }
  
  .share-section {
    padding: 24px;
  }
  
  .stats-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .share-buttons {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
  
  .action-buttons {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
}
```

Perfect! 🎨 I've completely redesigned the color palette to be much more harmonious and easier on the eyes. Here's what I changed:

## **🌈 New Balanced Color Palette:**

### **Primary Colors:**
- **Light Blue**: `#81d4fa` (softer cyan for main text/headings)
- **Blue**: `#64b5f6` (gentle blue for borders and accents)
- **Purple**: `#ce93d8` (muted lavender for secondary elements)
- **Deep Purple**: `#ab47bc` (rich purple for gradients)

### **Accent Colors:**
- **Soft Red**: `#ef5350` (much gentler than the harsh `#ff0040`)
- **Green**: `#66bb6a` (pleasant green instead of neon)
- **Orange**: `#ffa726` (warm orange for parking)
- **Light Blue**: `#4fc3f7` (sky blue for hints and info)

### **Removed Jarring Elements:**
- ❌ **Harsh neon red** (`#ff0040`) → ✅ **Soft coral red** (`#ef5350`)
- ❌ **Electric cyan** (`#00ffff`) → ✅ **Gentle light blue** (`#81d4fa`)
- ❌ **Shocking magenta** (`#ff00ff`) → ✅ **Muted lavender** (`#ce93d8`)
- ❌ **Neon green** (`#00ff80`) → ✅ **Natural green** (`#66bb6a`)

### **Visual Improvements:**
- **Softer glow effects** that don't strain the eyes
- **Harmonious gradients** using complementary colors
- **Better contrast** while maintaining readability
- **Subtle shadows** instead of harsh neon outlines

Now the interface maintains that futuristic, high-tech aesthetic but with a much more **pleasant and professional** appearance. The colors work together harmoniously instead of competing for attention, and the red elements (cars/roads) are noticeable without being jarring.

The result is a cyberpunk aesthetic that's actually comfortable to look at for extended periods! ✨

---

**User**

I like the electirc cyan and neon green, please keep them

---

**Cursor**

Got it! I'll bring back the electric cyan and neon green while keeping the softer red and other balanced colors. Let me restore those vibrant accents:

.game-start {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 20px;
}

.hero-section {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 40px;
  max-width: 800px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.3), 0 0 80px rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  text-align: center;
  color: white;
}

.title {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #00ffff;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.8);
}

.subtitle {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  color: #ce93d8;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.current-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 20px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.stat-card {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ab47bc 0%, #e91e63 100%);
  color: white;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border: 1px solid #ce93d8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.4);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  opacity: 0.9;
}

.preview-block {
  margin: 40px 0;
  text-align: left;
  background: #111111;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border: 1px solid #00ffff;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.preview-block h3 {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #00ffff;
  text-align: center;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.6);
}

.block-preview {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 12px;
}

.block-preview > div {
  background: #222222;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #f48fb1;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(244, 143, 177, 0.3);
}

.challenge-text {
  text-align: left;
  margin: 32px 0;
  background: #111111;
  padding: 24px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border-left: 4px solid #00ff80;
  border: 1px solid #00ff80;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.2);
}

.challenge-text p {
  margin-bottom: 12px;
  font-size: 1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.challenge-text p:last-child {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

.start-button {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00ffff 0%, #00ff80 100%);
  color: #000000;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 16px 32px;
  border-radius: 50px;
  margin-top: 24px;
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.6);
  text-shadow: none;
}

.start-button:hover {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00ff80 0%, #00ffff 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.8);

}

@media (max-width: 768px) {

  }

```css
.game-board {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: #000000;
  color: white;
  padding: 20px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.top-section {
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
  margin-bottom: 30px;
  align-items: stretch;
}

.stats-panel {
  background: #111111;
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 24px;
  min-width: 300px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.3);
}

.stats-panel h3 {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #00ffff;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.8);
}

.stats-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: 16px;
}

.stat-item {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 16px;
  background: #222222;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border-left: 4px solid #ce93d8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.3);
}

.stat-value {
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #ffffff;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  color: #ce93d8;
  margin-top: 4px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 1px;
}

.tools-panel {
  background: #111111;
  border: 2px solid #00ff80;
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 24px;
  flex: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.3);
}

.tools-panel h3 {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #00ff80;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.8);
}

.tools-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(140px, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
}

.tool-button {
  background: #222222;
  border: 2px solid #444444;
  color: white;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.tool-button.unlocked {
  border-color: #00ff80;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #003320 0%, #004d30 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.4);
}

.tool-button.unlocked:hover {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #004d30 0%, #00663f 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.6);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.tool-button.selected {
  border-color: #00ffff;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #003344 0%, #004455 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.5);
}

.main-section {
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
  flex: 1;
}

.city-block {
  background: #111111;
  border: 2px solid #ce93d8;
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 24px;
  flex: 2;
  min-height: 400px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.3);
}

.city-block h3 {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #ce93d8;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.8);
}

.block-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
  grid-template-rows: repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 8px;
  height: 300px;
  background: #222222;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border: 1px solid #444444;
}

.block-cell {
  background: #333333;
  border-radius: 4px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  color: white;
  border: 1px solid #555555;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.block-cell.filled {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ef5350 0%, #e53935 100%);
  border-color: #ef5350;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.4);
}

.block-cell.filled:hover {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e53935 0%, #d32f2f 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.6);
}

.hints-panel {
  background: #111111;
  border: 2px solid #ff9800;
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 24px;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 250px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.3);
}

.hints-panel h3 {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #ff9800;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.8);
}

.hint {
  background: #222222;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  border-left: 4px solid #ff9800;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  line-height: 1.5;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.2);
}

.hint:last-child {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

.finish-button {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00ffff 0%, #00ff80 100%);
  color: #000000;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 16px 32px;
  border-radius: 50px;
  margin-top: 20px;
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  align-self: center;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.6);
  text-shadow: none;
}

.finish-button:hover {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00ff80 0%, #00ffff 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.8);
  transform: translateY(-3px);
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-3px);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .top-section {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
  
  .main-section {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
  
  .stats-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .tools-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }
  
  .block-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    grid-template-rows: repeat(6, 1fr);
  }
}
```

```css
.game-results {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: #000000;
  color: white;
  padding: 20px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}

.results-container {
  max-width: 1000px;
  width: 100%;
  text-align: center;
}

.results-header {
  margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.results-title {
  font-size: 3rem;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  color: #00ffff;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.8);
}

.results-subtitle {
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  color: #ce93d8;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.improvement-percentage {
  font-size: 2rem;
  color: #00ff80;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.8);
}

.comparison-section {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 40px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.before-card, .after-card {
  background: #111111;
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 32px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
}

.before-card {
  border: 2px solid #ef5350;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.3);
}

.after-card {
  border: 2px solid #00ff80;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.3);
}

.card-title {
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.before-card .card-title {
  color: #ef5350;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.8);
}

.after-card .card-title {
  color: #00ff80;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.8);
}

.stats-comparison {
  display: grid;
  gap: 16px;
}

.stat-row {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  background: #222222;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid;
}

.before-card .stat-row {
  border-left-color: #ef5350;
}

.after-card .stat-row {
  border-left-color: #00ff80;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #ffffff;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.stat-value {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #ffffff;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
}

.insights-section {
  background: #111111;
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 32px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.3);
}

.insights-title {
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
  color: #00ffff;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.8);
}

.insights {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr));
  gap: 20px;
  text-align: left;
}

.insight {
  background: #222222;
  padding: 20px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border-left: 4px solid #ce93d8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.3);
}

.insight h4 {
  color: #ce93d8;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.insight p {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.actions-section {
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.action-button {
  padding: 16px 32px;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-radius: 50px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid;
  text-shadow: none;
}

.restart-button {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00ffff 0%, #00ff80 100%);
  color: #000000;
  border-color: #00ffff;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.6);
}

.restart-button:hover {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00ff80 0%, #00ffff 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.8);
  transform: translateY(-3px);
}

.share-button {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ce93d8 0%, #ab47bc 100%);
  color: #ffffff;
  border-color: #ce93d8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(206, 147, 216, 0.6);
}

.share-button:hover {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ab47bc 0%, #8e24aa 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(171, 71, 188, 0.8);
  transform: translateY(-3px);
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .results-title {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .comparison-section {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 20px;
  }
  
  .before-card, .after-card {
    padding: 24px;
  }
  
  .insights {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .actions-section {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .action-button {
    width: 200px;
  }
}
```

Perfect! I've restored the electric cyan (`#00ffff`) and neon green (`#00ff80`) colors while keeping the softer red (`#ef5350`) and other balanced colors. The app now has:

- **Electric cyan** for the main hero section border, titles, stats panel, and primary buttons
- **Neon green** for tools panel, challenge text section, finish button, and after-state results
- **Soft coral red** for car-related elements (much less jarring than the harsh red)
- **Muted lavender** for accent elements and hints
- **Natural colors** for other UI elements

The vibrant cyan and green really pop against the black background and create that energetic, tech-forward aesthetic you wanted, while the softer red is easier on the eyes for the car-related warnings. The game should feel both visually striking and comfortable to use!

.game-board {
  min-height: 100vh;

.game-header {

  padding: 24px;

}

.progress-bar {

}

}

}

}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #ffffff;
  margin-top: 4px;
}

.game-content {

  gap: 20px;

}

.tool-palette {
  background: #000000;
  border: 2px solid #00ff80;

.tool {

.tool.unlocked {

}

.tool.unlocked:hover {

}

.tool.locked {

}

.tool span:first-child {

}

.tool span:last-child {

}

.grid-item:hover {

}

.grid-item span:last-child {

.animations {

}

.people-animation {

  justify-content: center;
}

.person {

}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }

    transform: translateY(-5px);

}

.complete-btn {

## 🎮 Space Efficiency Game: Detailed Gameplay Design

### **Core Game Loop & Mechanics**

**Objective:** Transform a car-dependent city block into a thriving, people-first community through strategic placement and optimization.

### **Level Progression & Motives**

#### **Level 1: "The Car Trap" 🚗**
- **Motive:** Show players the futility of car-only optimization
- **Available Tools:** Cars, Parking Lots, Roads
- **Challenge:** "Optimize for cars - see how few people you can actually serve"
- **Player Actions:**
  - Drag/drop cars and parking spaces
  - Resize parking lots (10-100 spaces)
  - Adjust road width (2-8 lanes)
- **Scoring:** People served, revenue generated, space efficiency
- **Goal:** Achieve 50+ people (difficult with cars alone)
- **Revelation:** "You've hit the car ceiling - time to think differently!"

#### **Level 2: "The Bus Breakthrough" 🚌**
- **Motive:** Dramatic demonstration of mass transit efficiency
- **New Tools:** Buses, Bus Stops, Bus Lanes
- **Challenge:** "Add ONE bus line and watch the magic happen"
- **Player Actions:**
  - Place bus stops (affects catchment area)
  - Design bus route (straight, circular, express)
  - Choose bus frequency (every 2-15 minutes)
  - Convert car lanes to bus lanes
- **Scoring Multiplier:** 2x for transit usage
- **Goal:** Reach 200+ people
- **Revelation:** "One bus = 40 cars worth of people!"

#### **Level 3: "The Bike & Walk Revolution" 🚲🚶**
- **Motive:** Show how human-scale transport creates vibrant streets
- **New Tools:** Bike Lanes, Pedestrian Zones, Bike Share
- **Challenge:** "Make it safe and pleasant to walk and bike"
- **Player Actions:**
  - Create protected bike lanes
  - Design pedestrian plazas
  - Place bike share stations
  - Add street furniture (benches, trees)
- **Scoring Multiplier:** 3x for active transport
- **Goal:** Reach 400+ people
- **Revelation:** "Streets become places, not just throughways!"

#### **Level 4: "The Mixed-Use Miracle" 🏢🏠**
- **Motive:** Demonstrate how density creates convenience and community
- **New Tools:** Apartments, Mixed-Use Buildings, Pocket Parks
- **Challenge:** "Put homes above shops - instant customers!"
- **Player Actions:**
  - Build mixed-use developments (shops below, homes above)
  - Create pocket parks and green spaces
  - Design community spaces (plazas, markets)
  - Optimize building heights (2-8 stories)
- **Scoring Multiplier:** 4x for mixed-use synergy
- **Goal:** Reach 800+ people
- **Revelation:** "Density done right creates 15-minute neighborhoods!"

### **Detailed Scoring System**

#### **Primary Metrics:**
- **👥 People Capacity:** Core metric - how many people can live/work/visit
- **💰 Economic Activity:** Monthly revenue from businesses
- **🌱 Green Space %:** Parks, trees, nature vs. built area
- **😊 Happiness Index:** Composite of walkability, noise, air quality
- **⚡ Energy Efficiency:** Transport energy per person
- **🏠 Housing Units:** Residential capacity

#### **Scoring Multipliers:**
- **Car Dependency Penalty:** -50% for every car-parking space
- **Transit Bonus:** +100% for people using transit
- **Active Transport Bonus:** +200% for walking/biking
- **Mixed-Use Synergy:** +300% when housing is above retail
- **Green Space Bonus:** +50% for every 5% green space
- **Density Bonus:** +25% for every story above 2 floors

#### **Real-Time Feedback:**
- **People Counter:** Animated figures showing current capacity
- **Revenue Ticker:** Live updating as you make changes
- **Efficiency Meter:** People per square meter
- **Happiness Faces:** Visual mood indicator
- **Carbon Counter:** CO2 emissions per person

### **Interactive Actions & Tools**

#### **Drag & Drop Mechanics:**
- **Buildings:** Resize, rotate, stack floors
- **Transport:** Draw routes, adjust frequencies
- **Green Space:** Paint parks, place trees
- **Infrastructure:** Roads, utilities, lighting

#### **Advanced Tools (Unlocked by Level):**
- **Level 2+:** Bus route designer with real-time ridership
- **Level 3+:** Bike network planner with safety ratings
- **Level 4+:** Zoning tool for mixed-use optimization
- **Master Level:** Policy levers (parking prices, transit subsidies)

### **Gamification Elements**

#### **Achievement System:**
- 🏆 **"Car Slayer":** Replace 50+ parking spaces with housing
- 🚌 **"Transit Champion":** Get 80%+ of people using transit
- 🚲 **"Bike Whisperer":** Create 2+ miles of protected bike lanes
- 🌳 **"Green Warrior":** Achieve 30%+ green space
- 🏘️ **"Neighborhood Maker":** Build 5+ mixed-use buildings
- 📈 **"Economic Genius":** 10x the original revenue

#### **Leaderboard Categories:**
- **Most People:** Pure capacity optimization
- **Highest Revenue:** Economic efficiency
- **Greenest Block:** Environmental focus
- **Happiest Community:** Quality of life
- **Most Innovative:** Creative solutions

#### **Challenge Modes:**
- **Speed Round:** Optimize in 3 minutes
- **Budget Constraint:** Limited money to spend
- **Existing Infrastructure:** Work with what's there
- **Climate Crisis:** Optimize for zero emissions
- **Aging Population:** Accessibility-first design

### **Educational Integration**

#### **Real-World Data:**
- **Space Efficiency:** "A bus lane moves 40x more people than a car lane"
- **Economic Impact:** "Walkable streets have 2.5x higher property values"
- **Health Benefits:** "Car-free areas reduce childhood asthma by 50%"
- **Climate Impact:** "Transit produces 95% less CO2 per person than cars"

#### **City Case Studies:**
- **Copenhagen:** Bike infrastructure transformation
- **Curitiba:** Bus rapid transit innovation
- **Barcelona:** Superblocks and car-free zones
- **Singapore:** Mixed-use density done right

### **Difficulty Scaling**

#### **Beginner Mode:**
- Generous space and budget
- Clear optimal solutions
- Helpful hints and tutorials

#### **Expert Mode:**
- Realistic space constraints
- Budget limitations
- Political resistance simulation
- NIMBY (Not In My Backyard) challenges

#### **Master Mode:**
- Existing infrastructure constraints
- Stakeholder negotiations
- Long-term maintenance costs
- Climate change scenarios

```javascript
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import './GameBoard.css';

function GameBoard({ onComplete }) {
  const [level, setLevel] = useState(1);
  const [selectedTool, setSelectedTool] = useState(null);
  const [cityGrid, setCityGrid] = useState([
    { id: 1, type: 'road', content: '🛣️ 6-Lane Road', people: 0, revenue: 0, happiness: -10 },
    { id: 2, type: 'parking', content: '🅿️ Parking (50)', people: 12, revenue: 500, happiness: -5 },
    { id: 3, type: 'shops', content: '🏪 Strip Mall', people: 25, revenue: 8000, happiness: 5 },
    { id: 4, type: 'parking', content: '🅿️ Parking (30)', people: 10, revenue: 300, happiness: -3 },
  ]);
  
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({
    people: 47,
    revenue: 8800,
    greenSpace: 2,
    happiness: 35,
    efficiency: 12
  });

  const [achievements, setAchievements] = useState([]);

  const tools = {
    1: [
      { id: 'cars', name: 'Cars', icon: '🚗', people: 1, revenue: 0, happiness: -2, cost: 100 },
      { id: 'parking', name: 'Parking', icon: '🅿️', people: 0.3, revenue: 10, happiness: -1, cost: 200 },
      { id: 'road', name: 'Road', icon: '🛣️', people: 0, revenue: 0, happiness: -5, cost: 500 }
    ],
    2: [
      { id: 'bus', name: 'Bus Stop', icon: '🚌', people: 40, revenue: 200, happiness: 15, cost: 1000 },
      { id: 'buslane', name: 'Bus Lane', icon: '🚌', people: 60, revenue: 0, happiness: 10, cost: 800 }
    ],
    3: [
      { id: 'bike', name: 'Bike Lane', icon: '🚲', people: 20, revenue: 100, happiness: 20, cost: 300 },
      { id: 'pedestrian', name: 'Walkway', icon: '🚶', people: 30, revenue: 150, happiness: 25, cost: 400 },
      { id: 'park', name: 'Pocket Park', icon: '🌳', people: 15, revenue: 50, happiness: 30, cost: 600 }
    ],
    4: [
      { id: 'mixeduse', name: 'Mixed-Use', icon: '🏢', people: 80, revenue: 5000, happiness: 35, cost: 3000 },
      { id: 'apartments', name: 'Apartments', icon: '🏠', people: 120, revenue: 2000, happiness: 20, cost: 4000 }
    ]
  };

  const levelData = {
    1: { 
      name: "The Car Trap", 
      goal: 60, 
      hint: "Try optimizing with cars... notice the limitation?",
      unlocked: tools[1] 
    },
    2: { 
      name: "Bus Breakthrough", 
      goal: 200, 
      hint: "Add ONE bus stop and watch the magic happen!",
      unlocked: [...tools[1], ...tools[2]] 
    },
    3: { 
      name: "Bike & Walk Revolution", 
      goal: 400, 
      hint: "Make it safe and pleasant for people to walk and bike",
      unlocked: [...tools[1], ...tools[2], ...tools[3]] 
    },
    4: { 
      name: "Mixed-Use Miracle", 
      goal: 800, 
      hint: "Put homes above shops - instant customers!",
      unlocked: [...tools[1], ...tools[2], ...tools[3], ...tools[4]] 
    }
  };

  // Calculate stats based on city grid
  useEffect(() => {
    const newStats = cityGrid.reduce((acc, cell) => {
      const multiplier = getMultiplier(cell.type);
      return {
        people: acc.people + (cell.people * multiplier),
        revenue: acc.revenue + (cell.revenue * multiplier),
        happiness: acc.happiness + cell.happiness,
        greenSpace: acc.greenSpace + (cell.type === 'park' ? 8 : 0)
      };
    }, { people: 0, revenue: 0, happiness: 50, greenSpace: 2 });

    newStats.efficiency = Math.round(newStats.people / cityGrid.length);
    setStats(newStats);

    // Check achievements
    checkAchievements(newStats);
  }, [cityGrid, level]);

  const getMultiplier = (type) => {
    const multipliers = {
      'cars': 0.5,      // Car penalty
      'parking': 0.3,   // Parking penalty  
      'road': 0.1,      // Road penalty
      'bus': 2.0,       // Transit bonus
      'buslane': 2.5,   // Transit bonus
      'bike': 3.0,      // Active transport bonus
      'pedestrian': 3.5, // Active transport bonus
      'park': 2.0,      // Green bonus
      'mixeduse': 4.0,  // Mixed-use synergy
      'apartments': 3.0  // Density bonus
    };
    return multipliers[type] || 1.0;
  };

  const checkAchievements = (newStats) => {
    const newAchievements = [];
    
    if (newStats.people > 100 && !achievements.includes('first-hundred')) {
      newAchievements.push('first-hundred');
    }
    if (newStats.people > 500 && !achievements.includes('transit-champion')) {
      newAchievements.push('transit-champion');
    }
    if (newStats.greenSpace > 20 && !achievements.includes('green-warrior')) {
      newAchievements.push('green-warrior');
    }
    if (newStats.revenue > 20000 && !achievements.includes('economic-genius')) {
      newAchievements.push('economic-genius');
    }

    if (newAchievements.length > 0) {
      setAchievements([...achievements, ...newAchievements]);
    }
  };

  const handleCellClick = (cellId) => {
    if (!selectedTool) return;

    setCityGrid(prev => prev.map(cell => 
      cell.id === cellId 
        ? { 
            ...cell, 
            type: selectedTool.id,
            content: `${selectedTool.icon} ${selectedTool.name}`,
            people: selectedTool.people,
            revenue: selectedTool.revenue,
            happiness: selectedTool.happiness
          }
        : cell
    ));
  };

  const addNewCell = () => {
    if (!selectedTool || cityGrid.length >= 12) return;

    const newCell = {
      id: cityGrid.length + 1,
      type: selectedTool.id,
      content: `${selectedTool.icon} ${selectedTool.name}`,
      people: selectedTool.people,
      revenue: selectedTool.revenue,
      happiness: selectedTool.happiness
    };

    setCityGrid([...cityGrid, newCell]);
  };

  const canAdvanceLevel = () => {
    return stats.people >= levelData[level].goal;
  };

  const handleNextLevel = () => {
    if (canAdvanceLevel() && level < 4) {
      setLevel(level + 1);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="game-board">
      {/* Header Stats */}
      <div className="game-header">
        <div className="level-info">
          <h2>Level {level}: {levelData[level].name}</h2>
          <div className="progress-bar">
            <div className="progress" style={{width: `${(level/4) * 100}%`}}></div>
          </div>
          <div className="level-goal">
            Goal: {levelData[level].goal} people 
            {canAdvanceLevel() && <span className="goal-achieved"> ✅ ACHIEVED!</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="live-stats">
          <div className="stat">
            <span className="stat-value">{Math.round(stats.people)}</span>
            <span className="stat-label">👥 People</span>
          </div>
          <div className="stat">
            <span className="stat-value">${Math.round(stats.revenue/1000)}K</span>
            <span className="stat-label">💰 Revenue</span>
          </div>
          <div className="stat">
            <span className="stat-value">{Math.round(stats.greenSpace)}%</span>
            <span className="stat-label">🌱 Green</span>
          </div>
          <div className="stat">
            <span className="stat-value">{Math.round(stats.happiness)}%</span>
            <span className="stat-label">😊 Happy</span>
          </div>
          <div className="stat">
            <span className="stat-value">{stats.efficiency}</span>
            <span className="stat-label">⚡ Efficiency</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="game-content">
        {/* Tool Palette */}
        <div className="tool-palette">
          <h3>🎮 Tools</h3>
          <div className="tools">
            {levelData[level].unlocked.map(tool => (
              <div 
                key={tool.id}
                className={`tool unlocked ${selectedTool?.id === tool.id ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setSelectedTool(tool)}
              >
                <span className="tool-icon">{tool.icon}</span>
                <span className="tool-name">{tool.name}</span>
                <div className="tool-stats">
                  <div>👥 {tool.people}</div>
                  <div>💰 ${tool.revenue}</div>
                  <div>😊 {tool.happiness > 0 ? '+' : ''}{tool.happiness}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          
          <div className="tool-actions">
            <button 
              className="add-cell-btn"
              onClick={addNewCell}
              disabled={!selectedTool || cityGrid.length >= 12}
            >
              + Add New Space
            </button>
            
            {canAdvanceLevel() && level < 4 && (
              <button 
                className="next-level-btn achieved"
                onClick={handleNextLevel}
              >
                Next Level →
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Main Game Area */}
        <div className="city-block">
          <h3>📱 Your City Block</h3>
          <div className="instructions">
            {selectedTool ? 
              `Click any space to place ${selectedTool.name} or add new space` : 
              'Select a tool from the palette to start building'
            }
          </div>
          
          <div className="block-grid">
            {cityGrid.map(cell => (
              <div 
                key={cell.id}
                className={`grid-item ${cell.type} ${selectedTool ? 'clickable' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => handleCellClick(cell.id)}
              >
                <div className="cell-content">{cell.content}</div>
                <div className="cell-stats">
                  <span>👥 {Math.round(cell.people * getMultiplier(cell.type))}</span>
                  <span>💰 ${Math.round(cell.revenue * getMultiplier(cell.type))}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          
          <div className="people-animation">
            {Array.from({length: Math.min(50, Math.floor(stats.people/20))}).map((_, i) => (
              <span key={i} className="person" style={{
                animationDelay: `${i * 0.1}s`,
                left: `${(i * 15) % 100}%`
              }}>🚶</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Hints & Achievements */}
        <div className="hints-panel">
          <h3>💡 Current Challenge</h3>
          <div className="hint">{levelData[level].hint}</div>
          
          {achievements.length > 0 && (
            <div className="achievements">
              <h4>🏆 Achievements</h4>
              {achievements.map(achievement => (
                <div key={achievement} className="achievement">
                  {achievement === 'first-hundred' && '🎯 First Hundred!'}
                  {achievement === 'transit-champion' && '🚌 Transit Champion!'}
                  {achievement === 'green-warrior' && '🌳 Green Warrior!'}
                  {achievement === 'economic-genius' && '💰 Economic Genius!'}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          
          {level === 4 && canAdvanceLevel() && (
            <button className="complete-btn" onClick={onComplete}>
              See Final Results →
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default GameBoard;
```

Now let me update the CSS to support the new interactive elements:

```css
/* GameBoard.css */
.game-board {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0a0a0a 0%, #1a0a1a 50%, #0a1a1a 100%);
  color: #ffffff;
  padding: 20px;
}

.game-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: flex-start;
  margin-bottom: 30px;
  padding: 20px;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.3);
}

.level-info h2 {
  color: #00ffff;
  margin: 0 0 10px 0;
  font-size: 1.8em;
}

.progress-bar {
  width: 300px;
  height: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-radius: 4px;
  overflow: hidden;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.progress {
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #00ffff, #00ff80);
  transition: width 0.5s ease;
}

.level-goal {
  font-size: 1.1em;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.goal-achieved {
  color: #00ff80;
  font-weight: bold;
  animation: pulse 1s infinite;
}

@keyframes pulse {
  0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
  50% { opacity: 0.7; }
}

.live-stats {
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
}

.stat {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 10px 15px;
  background: rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid #00ffff;
  border-radius: 10px;
}

.stat-value {
  display: block;
  font-size: 1.5em;
  font-weight: bold;
  color: #00ff80;
}

.stat-label {
  display: block;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  color: #cccccc;
  margin-top: 5px;
}

.game-content {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 300px 1fr 250px;
  gap: 30px;
  align-items: start;
}

/* Tool Palette */
.tool-palette {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);
  border: 2px solid #ff00ff;
  border-radius: 15px;
  padding: 20px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(255, 0, 255, 0.2);
}

.tool-palette h3 {
  color: #ff00ff;
  margin: 0 0 15px 0;
  text-align: center;
}

.tools {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.tool {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 15px;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.tool.unlocked {
  border-color: #00ff80;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.tool.unlocked:hover {
  background: rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.2);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.3);
}

.tool.selected {
  background: rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.3);
  border-color: #00ffff;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.5);
}

.tool-icon {
  font-size: 2em;
  margin-bottom: 5px;
}

.tool-name {
  font-weight: bold;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  color: #00ff80;
}

.tool-stats {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 3px;
  font-size: 0.8em;
  color: #cccccc;
  text-align: center;
}

.tool-actions {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 10px;
}

.add-cell-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(45deg, #00ff80, #00ffff);
  color: #000000;
  border: none;
  padding: 12px 20px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-weight: bold;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.add-cell-btn:hover:not(:disabled) {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.4);
}

.add-cell-btn:disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.next-level-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(45deg, #ff00ff, #00ffff);
  color: #ffffff;
  border: none;
  padding: 15px 25px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  font-weight: bold;
  font-size: 1.1em;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.next-level-btn.achieved {
  animation: glow 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes glow {
  0%, 100% { box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(255, 0, 255, 0.5); }
  50% { box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.8); }
}

/* City Block */
.city-block {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  border: 2px solid #00ffff;
  border-radius: 15px;
  padding: 20px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.2);
  position: relative;
}

.city-block h3 {
  color: #00ffff;
  margin: 0 0 15px 0;
  text-align: center;
}

.instructions {
  text-align: center;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  padding: 10px;
  background: rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: #00ffff;
  font-weight: bold;
}

.block-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
  gap: 15px;
  margin-bottom: 30px;
}

.grid-item {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border: 2px solid #666666;
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 15px;
  text-align: center;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  min-height: 120px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.grid-item.clickable {
  cursor: pointer;
  border-color: #00ff80;
}

.grid-item.clickable:hover {
  background: rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.2);
  transform: translateY(-3px);
  box-shadow: 0 5px 20px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.3);
}

.grid-item.road {
  border-color: #ef5350;
  background: rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.1);
}

.grid-item.parking {
  border-color: #ff9800;
  background: rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.1);
}

.grid-item.shops {
  border-color: #00ff80;
  background: rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.1);
}

.grid-item.bus, .grid-item.buslane {
  border-color: #2196f3;
  background: rgba(33, 150, 243, 0.1);
}

.grid-item.bike, .grid-item.pedestrian {
  border-color: #00ffff;
  background: rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.grid-item.park {
  border-color: #4caf50;
  background: rgba(76, 175, 80, 0.1);
}

.grid-item.mixeduse, .grid-item.apartments {
  border-color: #ff00ff;
  background: rgba(255, 0, 255, 0.1);
}

.cell-content {
  font-size: 1.1em;
  font-weight: bold;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.cell-stats {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-around;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  color: #cccccc;
  margin-top: auto;
}

.people-animation {
  position: relative;
  height: 40px;
  overflow: hidden;
  margin-top: 20px;
}

.person {
  position: absolute;
  font-size: 1.2em;
  animation: walk 3s linear infinite;
}

@keyframes walk {
  0% { transform: translateX(-20px); }
  100% { transform: translateX(calc(100vw - 100px)); }
}

/* Hints Panel */
.hints-panel {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);
  border: 2px solid #00ff80;
  border-radius: 15px;
  padding: 20px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.2);
}

.hints-panel h3 {
  color: #00ff80;
  margin: 0 0 15px 0;
}

.hint {
  background: rgba(0, 255, 128, 0.1);
  padding: 15px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  border-left: 4px solid #00ff80;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.achievements {
  margin-top: 20px;
}

.achievements h4 {
  color: #ffff00;
  margin: 0 0 10px 0;
}

.achievement {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 0, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid #ffff00;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  animation: achievementPop 0.5s ease;
}

@keyframes achievementPop {
  0% { transform: scale(0.8); opacity: 0; }
  100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
}

.complete-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(45deg, #ff00ff, #00ffff);
  color: #ffffff;
  border: none;
  padding: 15px 25px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  font-weight: bold;
  font-size: 1.1em;
  cursor: pointer;
  width: 100%;
  margin-top: 20px;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.complete-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 5px 20px rgba(255, 0, 255, 0.4);
}

/* Responsive Design */
@media (max-width: 1200px) {
  .game-content {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 20px;
  }
  
  .game-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 20px;
  }
  
  .live-stats {
    justify-content: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .game-board {
    padding: 10px;
  }
  
  .block-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .tools {
    flex-direction: row;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
  
  .tool {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: calc(50% - 5px);
  }
}
```

Now let me update the GameResults to reflect the new dynamic scoring system:

```javascript
import React from 'react';
import './GameResults.css';

function GameResults({ onRestart, gameStats, achievements }) {
  const results = {
    before: {
      people: 47,
      revenue: 8800,
      greenSpace: 2,
      happiness: 35,
      businesses: 3
    },
    after: {
      people: gameStats?.people || 847,
      revenue: gameStats?.revenue || 52000,
      greenSpace: gameStats?.greenSpace || 25,
      happiness: gameStats?.happiness || 87,
      businesses: Math.floor((gameStats?.people || 847) / 70) // Estimate businesses based on people
    }
  };

  const improvement = {
    people: Math.round(((results.after.people - results.before.people) / results.before.people) * 100),
    revenue: Math.round(((results.after.revenue - results.before.revenue) / results.before.revenue) * 100),
    greenSpace: results.after.greenSpace - results.before.greenSpace,
    happiness: results.after.happiness - results.before.happiness
  };

  const getAchievementText = (achievement) => {
    const texts = {
      'first-hundred': '🎯 First Hundred People!',
      'transit-champion': '🚌 Transit Champion!',
      'green-warrior': '🌳 Green Warrior!',
      'economic-genius': '💰 Economic Genius!'
    };
    return texts[achievement] || achievement;
  };

  const getShareText = () => {
    return `I transformed a car-dependent block into a thriving community! 🏙️
🎯 Fit ${results.after.people} people where only ${results.before.people} lived before (+${improvement.people}% improvement!)
💰 Boosted local business revenue by ${improvement.revenue}%
🌱 Increased green space by ${improvement.greenSpace} percentage points
😊 Happiness increased by ${improvement.happiness} points

Play the Space Efficiency Challenge and see how much better cities can be without car dependency!`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="game-results">
      <div className="results-header">
        <h1>🎉 Transformation Complete!</h1>
        <p className="subtitle">Look what you accomplished by ditching car dependency</p>
      </div>

      <div className="comparison-section">
        <div className="before-after">
          <div className="comparison-column">
            <h2>🚗 Before: Car-Dependent</h2>
            <div className="city-visual before">
              <div className="visual-item">🛣️ 6-Lane Road</div>
              <div className="visual-item">🅿️ Parking Lot (50 spaces)</div>
              <div className="visual-item">🏪 Strip Mall</div>
              <div className="visual-item">🅿️ More Parking (30 spaces)</div>
            </div>
            <div className="stats-grid">
              <div className="stat-item">
                <span className="number">{results.before.people}</span>
                <span className="label">👥 People</span>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-item">
                <span className="number">${Math.round(results.before.revenue/1000)}K</span>
                <span className="label">💰 Revenue</span>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-item">
                <span className="number">{results.before.greenSpace}%</span>
                <span className="label">🌱 Green</span>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-item">
                <span className="number">{results.before.happiness}%</span>
                <span className="label">😊 Happy</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="vs-arrow">→</div>

          <div className="comparison-column">
            <h2>🌟 After: People-First</h2>
            <div className="city-visual after">
              <div className="visual-item">🚌 Bus Rapid Transit</div>
              <div className="visual-item">🌳 Pocket Parks + Cafes</div>
              <div className="visual-item">🏢 Mixed-Use Buildings</div>
              <div className="visual-item">🚲 Protected Bike Lanes</div>
            </div>
            <div className="stats-grid">
              <div className="stat-item improved">
                <span className="number">{Math.round(results.after.people)}</span>
                <span className="label">👥 People</span>
                <span className="improvement">+{improvement.people}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-item improved">
                <span className="number">${Math.round(results.after.revenue/1000)}K</span>
                <span className="label">💰 Revenue</span>
                <span className="improvement">+{improvement.revenue}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-item improved">
                <span className="number">{Math.round(results.after.greenSpace)}%</span>
                <span className="label">🌱 Green</span>
                <span className="improvement">+{improvement.greenSpace}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-item improved">
                <span className="number">{Math.round(results.after.happiness)}%</span>
                <span className="label">😊 Happy</span>
                <span className="improvement">+{improvement.happiness}%</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Show earned achievements */}
      {achievements && achievements.length > 0 && (
        <div className="achievements-section">
          <h2>🏆 Achievements Earned</h2>
          <div className="achievements-grid">
            {achievements.map(achievement => (
              <div key={achievement} className="achievement-card">
                {getAchievementText(achievement)}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="key-insights">
        <h2>💡 Key Insights</h2>
        <div className="insights-grid">
          <div className="insight-card">
            <h3>🚀 Space Efficiency</h3>
            <p>You fit <strong>{Math.round(results.after.people / results.before.people)}x more people</strong> in the same space by prioritizing efficient transport</p>
          </div>
          <div className="insight-card">
            <h3>💰 Economic Boom</h3>
            <p>Local businesses saw <strong>{improvement.revenue}% revenue increase</strong> from foot traffic</p>
          </div>
          <div className="insight-card">
            <h3>🌱 Livability</h3>
            <p>Green space increased by <strong>{improvement.greenSpace} percentage points</strong>, happiness by <strong>{improvement.happiness} points</strong></p>
          </div>
          <div className="insight-card">
            <h3>🏘️ Community</h3>
            <p>From {results.before.businesses} isolated shops to {results.after.businesses} thriving businesses with residents nearby</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="share-section">
        <h2>📢 Share Your Results</h2>
        <div className="share-text">
          <p><strong>"I transformed a car-dependent block into a thriving community!"</strong></p>
          <p>🎯 Fit {Math.round(results.after.people)} people where only {results.before.people} lived before (+{improvement.people}% improvement!)</p>
          <p>💰 Boosted local business revenue by {improvement.revenue}%</p>
          <p>🌱 Increased green space by {improvement.greenSpace} percentage points</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="share-buttons">
          <button 
            className="share-btn twitter"
            onClick={() => {
              const text = encodeURIComponent(getShareText());
              window.open(`https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${text}`, '_blank');
            }}
          >
            Share on Twitter
          </button>
          <button 
            className="share-btn facebook"
            onClick={() => {
              const url = encodeURIComponent(window.location.href);
              window.open(`https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${url}`, '_blank');
            }}
          >
            Share on Facebook
          </button>
          <button 
            className="share-btn copy"
            onClick={() => {
              navigator.clipboard.writeText(getShareText());
              alert('Results copied to clipboard!');
            }}
          >
            Copy Results
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="action-buttons">
        <button className="restart-btn" onClick={onRestart}>
          Try Again
        </button>
        <button className="learn-more-btn" onClick={() => {
          window.open('https://www.strongtowns.org/the-growth-ponzi-scheme', '_blank');
        }}>
          Learn More About Car-Free Cities
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default GameResults;
```

Now let me update the main App.js to pass the game stats and achievements to the GameResults component:

```javascript
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import './App.css';
import GameStart from './components/GameStart';
import GameBoard from './components/GameBoard';
import GameResults from './components/GameResults';

function App() {
  const [currentScreen, setCurrentScreen] = useState('start');
  const [gameStats, setGameStats] = useState(null);
  const [achievements, setAchievements] = useState([]);
  
  const handleGameComplete = (finalStats, earnedAchievements) => {
    setGameStats(finalStats);
    setAchievements(earnedAchievements);
    setCurrentScreen('results');
  };

  const handleRestart = () => {
    setGameStats(null);
    setAchievements([]);
    setCurrentScreen('start');
  };
  
  const screens = {
    start: <GameStart onStart={() => setCurrentScreen('game')} />,
    game: <GameBoard onComplete={handleGameComplete} />,
    results: <GameResults 
      onRestart={handleRestart} 
      gameStats={gameStats}
      achievements={achievements}
    />
  };

  return (
    <div className="App">
      {screens[currentScreen]}
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

Perfect! 🎉 Your **Space Efficiency Game MVP** is now fully functional with all the core gameplay mechanics we discussed! Here's what I've implemented:

## 🎮 **Working MVP Features**

### **Interactive Gameplay:**
- **Tool Selection:** Click tools in the palette to select them
- **Drag & Drop:** Click any city block cell to place selected tools
- **Dynamic Building:** Add new spaces with the "Add New Space" button
- **Real-time Stats:** Live updating people count, revenue, green space, happiness, and efficiency

### **Level Progression System:**
- **Level 1 "The Car Trap":** Only cars/parking available - shows the limitation (goal: 60 people)
- **Level 2 "Bus Breakthrough":** Unlocks buses - dramatic improvement (goal: 200 people)  
- **Level 3 "Bike & Walk Revolution":** Adds bikes/walking/parks (goal: 400 people)
- **Level 4 "Mixed-Use Miracle":** Full toolkit with apartments/mixed-use (goal: 800 people)

### **Advanced Scoring System:**
- **Multiplier Effects:** Cars get penalties, transit gets 2x bonus, bikes get 3x, mixed-use gets 4x
- **Real-time Calculations:** Stats update instantly as you place tools
- **Efficiency Metric:** People per space shows optimization

### **Achievement System:**
- 🎯 **First Hundred:** Reach 100+ people
- 🚌 **Transit Champion:** Reach 500+ people  
- 🌳 **Green Warrior:** Get 20%+ green space
- 💰 **Economic Genius:** Generate $20K+ revenue
- Achievements pop up with animations when earned

### **Dynamic Results:**
- **Real Stats:** Final results show YOUR actual performance
- **Personalized Insights:** Calculations based on your choices
- **Achievement Display:** Shows what you actually earned
- **Working Share Buttons:** Twitter, Facebook, copy-to-clipboard

### **Visual Polish:**
- **Cyberpunk Aesthetic:** Electric cyan, neon green, softer red
- **Interactive Animations:** Walking people, glowing buttons, hover effects
- **Responsive Design:** Works on mobile and desktop
- **Clear Visual Feedback:** Selected tools, clickable areas, goal achievements

## 🚀 **How to Play:**

1. **Start** with the car-dependent block (47 people)
2. **Select tools** from the palette (unlocked by level)
3. **Click city spaces** to place tools or add new ones
4. **Watch stats** update in real-time
5. **Achieve level goals** to unlock new tools
6. **Earn achievements** through smart city planning
7. **Complete Level 4** to see your transformation results!

The game now viscerally demonstrates your core argument: **car-dependent design is fundamentally space-inefficient compared to people-first urban planning**. Players experience the "slap in the face" moment when they realize how much better cities can be!

Your MVP is running at `http://localhost:3000` - try it out and see how the interactive mechanics feel! 🏙️✨

Perfect! 🎉 I've added a **Reset Block** feature to your game! Here's what's new:

## 🔄 **Reset Functionality Added:**

### **Reset Button:**
- **Location:** In the tool palette alongside "Add New Space" and "Next Level"
- **Styling:** Orange gradient with hover effects to stand out
- **Icon:** 🔄 for clear visual indication

### **Reset Behavior:**
- **Restores Original Block:** Returns to the starting car-dependent layout
- **Clears Tool Selection:** Deselects any currently selected tool  
- **Instant Reset:** Stats immediately recalculate to starting values
- **Works at Any Level:** Players can reset and try different approaches

### **Why This Matters:**
- **Experimentation:** Players can try different optimization strategies
- **Learning:** Compare different approaches side-by-side
- **Frustration Relief:** No need to restart entire game if stuck
- **Strategic Planning:** Test ideas without commitment

### **Also Fixed:**
- **ESLint Warning:** Used `useCallback` for the `checkAchievements` function to resolve the React Hook dependency warning

## 🎮 **Updated Gameplay Flow:**

1. **Build** your city block with available tools
2. **Watch** stats update in real-time  
3. **Reset** if you want to try a different approach
4. **Experiment** with various combinations
5. **Achieve** level goals to progress
6. **Complete** Level 4 to see final results

The reset button uses a warm orange gradient (`#ff6b6b` to `#ff8e53`) to differentiate it from the green "Add Space" and purple "Next Level" buttons, making it easy to spot when players want to start over with their city block design.

Your MVP now has complete gameplay flexibility - players can iterate and experiment freely! 🏙️✨

## 🏪 **Level 1: Much Clearer Now!**

### **What You're Looking At:**
A **typical American strip mall** with clear explanations:

1. **🛣️ 6-Lane Highway**
   - *"Moves cars, not people"*
   - Shows 0 people (highways don't serve people directly)

2. **🅿️ Surface Parking Lot** 
   - *"50 cars = ~50 customers/day"*
   - Shows the car-to-customer relationship

3. **🏪 Strip Mall**
   - *"3 shops, car-dependent customers"*
   - The actual businesses generating revenue

4. **🅿️ More Parking**
   - *"30 cars = ~30 customers/day"*
   - More space dedicated to car storage

### **Key Educational Elements:**

**📚 Level Explanation Box:**
> *"What you're looking at: A typical American strip mall. Notice how much space is dedicated to cars (road + parking) vs. people (shops). The 'people' count shows daily visitors, not residents."*

**🎯 Updated Challenge:**
- **Goal:** 80 people (slightly higher to show the limitation)
- **Hint:** *"This is a typical car-dependent strip mall. Most space goes to moving & storing cars, not people! Try rearranging - notice how few people you can actually serve?"*

### **The "Aha!" Moment:**
Players now clearly see that:
- **75% of space** = moving/storing cars
- **25% of space** = actual businesses  
- **Only 80 people total** can be served efficiently
- **Parking lots** are just car storage, not productive space

This makes the car-dependency problem visceral and obvious before they unlock better alternatives! 🚗➡️🚌🚲🏢

Perfect! 🏙️ Now you have a **much more relatable and realistic city block** instead of a strip mall! Here's what players will see:

## 🏢 **Level 1: Real US City Block**

### **What You're Looking At Now:**
A **typical downtown city block** that people recognize from their daily lives:

1. **🛣️ 4-Lane Street** 
   - *"Main street with car traffic"*
   - The primary arterial road

2. **🅿️ Street Parking**
   - *"40 metered spots"*
   - On-street parking that takes up road space

3. **🏢 Ground Floor Retail**
   - *"Cafes, shops, restaurants"*
   - Active street-level businesses

4. **🅿️ Parking Garage**
   - *"200-space garage (3 floors)"*
   - Multi-story car storage taking valuable urban space

5. **🛣️ Side Street**
   - *"2-lane residential street"*
   - Secondary road for local access

6. **🏢 Office Building**
   - *"5-story office (200 workers)"*
   - Productive workspace

### **Key Improvements:**

**📊 Realistic Stats:**
- **500 people** daily capacity (workers + customers)
- **$21K monthly revenue** from businesses
- **0% green space** (typical urban reality)

**🎯 Updated Challenge:**
- **Goal:** 600 people (shows car infrastructure limitation)
- **Hint:** *"This is a typical US city block. Half the space is roads & parking! Try rearranging with cars only - notice the limitation of car-dependent design."*

**🏙️ Relatable Context:**
Players now see a block they recognize from:
- Downtown areas they visit
- Their commute to work
- Shopping districts they know

### **The Urban Planning Reality:**
This shows the **real problem** with American cities:
- **~50% of space** = moving & storing cars
- **~50% of space** = productive uses (offices, retail)
- **0% green space** = concrete jungle
- **Limited capacity** despite high density

This makes the car-dependency problem much more visceral because players think: *"Wait, this looks like my downtown... and it's mostly parking?!"* 

The transformation through the levels will be even more dramatic now! 🚗➡️🚌🚲🏢🌳

}

Perfect! 🗺️ Now you have a **proper bird's eye view city block** that makes much more sense! Here's what players will see:

## 🏙️ **Bird's Eye View City Block Layout**

### **The 3x3 Grid Layout:**
```
🛣️ North St  | 🛣️ East St   | 🛣️ South St
🛣️ West St   | 🅿️ Surface   | 🏢 Retail
🏗️ Vacant    | 🏢 Office    | 🅿️ Structure
```

### **What Each Space Represents:**

**🛣️ Roads (4 spaces):**
- **North & South Streets:** Main 4-lane arterials
- **East & West Streets:** 2-lane side streets
- Form the perimeter that defines the city block

**🏢 Buildings (2 spaces):**
- **Retail Strip:** Ground floor shops (80 people, $15K revenue)
- **Office Building:** 6-story office (250 workers, $12K revenue)

**🅿️ Parking (2 spaces):**
- **Surface Parking:** Large lot (150 cars, minimal revenue)
- **Parking Structure:** 4-level garage (300 cars, some revenue)

**🏗️ Vacant Lot (1 space):**
- Empty lot currently used for overflow parking
- Represents underutilized urban land

### **Key Urban Planning Insights:**

**📊 Block Analysis:**
- **44% of spaces** = roads (necessary infrastructure)
- **22% of spaces** = productive buildings 
- **22% of spaces** = parking (car storage)
- **11% of spaces** = vacant/underused

**🎯 The Challenge:**
- **Current capacity:** 830 people
- **Goal:** 900 people (barely achievable with cars only)
- Shows how car infrastructure severely limits urban potential

### **Why This Works Better:**

1. **Realistic Layout:** Matches how real city blocks are organized
2. **Spatial Understanding:** Players see the true cost of car infrastructure
3. **Urban Planning Logic:** Roads form perimeter, buildings fill interior
4. **Scalable Design:** Easy to understand how improvements multiply across a city

Players now think: *"Wait, half this block is just for moving and storing cars? What if we used that space for people instead?"*

The transformation through levels will be even more dramatic! 🚗➡️🚌🚲🏢🌳

```plaintext
🛣️ North St  | 🛣️ East St   | 🛣️ South St
🛣️ West St   | 🅿️ Surface   | 🏢 Retail
🏗️ Vacant    | 🏢 Office    | 🅿️ Structure
```
